# O 開頭詞條

> 本檔收錄 2,299 條含中文對譯的詞目。

<a id="entry-o-000001"></a>
## o

*n.* 1. **氧** — a nonmetallic bivalent element that is normally a colorless odorless tasteless nonflammable diatomic gas; constitutes 21 percent of the atmosphere by volume; the most abundant element in the earth's crust
2. the 15th letter of the Roman alphabet
3. the blood group whose red cells carry neither the A nor B antigens
*“people with type O blood are universal donors”*

<a id="entry-o-000002"></a>
## o level

*n.* 1. **O級, 普通水平, 普通程度** — the basic level of a subject taken in school

<a id="entry-o-000003"></a>
## o'clock

**發音：** /əˈklɒk/
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable, with-numeral)* **一點鐘 /一點鐘** — In conjunction with a numeral, indicates the direction, relative to the speaker or a vehicle, especially an aircraft, corresponding to the direction the hour hand is pointing at the time corresponding to the numeral, with twelve representing directly ahead on a horizontal plane, or directly up on a vertical plane, and three being to the right on either.
2. according to the clock
*“it's three o'clock in Tokyo now”*

<a id="entry-o-000004"></a>
## O'Connor

*prop. n.* 1. **奧康納 /奧康納** — A patrilineal surname from Irish.

<a id="entry-o-000005"></a>
## O'Malley

**發音：** /oʊˈmæli/
*prop. n.* 1. **奧馬利 /奧馬利** — A surname from Irish.

<a id="entry-o-000006"></a>
## O-ring

*n.* 1. **o型環 /o型環** — A circular gasket, usually made of rubber.

<a id="entry-o-000007"></a>
## o.k.

*adj.* 1. **令人滿意+的, 可以接受+的, 好+的, 很好+的, 說得過去+的** — being satisfactory or in satisfactory condition
*“an all-right movie”*
*“the passengers were shaken up but are all right”*
*n.* 1. an endorsement
*“they gave us the O.K. to go ahead”*
*v.* 1. **通過, 批准** — give sanction to
*“I approve of his educational policies”*
*adv.* 1. in a satisfactory or adequate manner
*“she'll do okay on her own”*
*“held up all right under pressure; (`alright' is a nonstandard variant of `all right')”*

<a id="entry-o-000008"></a>
## oafish

*adj.* 1. **舉止粗魯的, 粗野的, 無禮的, 粗魯的, 魯莽的** — ill-mannered and coarse and contemptible in behavior or appearance
*“was boorish and insensitive”*
*“the loutish manners of a bully”*

## oak

**發音：** /əʊk/
*n.* 1. **櫟樹, 橡木, 橡樹** — a deciduous tree of the genus Quercus; has acorns and lobed leaves
*“great oaks grow from little acorns”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **橡樹 /橡樹** — Any tree of the genus Quercus, in family Fagaceae.
3. the hard durable wood of any oak; used especially for furniture and flooring

*“great oaks grow from little acorns”*

## oar

**發音：** /ɔː/
*n.* 1. **槳 /槳** — A type of lever used to propel a boat, having a flat blade at one end and a handle at the other, and pivoted in a rowlock atop the gunwale, whereby a rower seated in the boat and pulling the handle can pass the blade through the water by repeated strokes against the water's resistance, thus moving the boat.
2. an implement used to propel or steer a boat

2. **避難所** — a shelter serving as a place of safety or sanctuary
3. a fertile tract in a desert (where the water table approaches the surface)

## oat

2. annual grass of Europe and North Africa; grains used as food and fodder (referred to primarily in the plural: `oats')
3. seed of the annual grass Avena sativa (spoken of primarily in the plural as `oats')

## oat cell carcinoma

*n.* 1. **燕麥細胞瘤** — highly malignant carcinoma composed of small round or egg-shaped cells with little cytoplasm; lung cancers are frequently oat cell carcinomas

<a id="entry-o-000026"></a>
## oath

**發音：** /ˈəʊθ/
*n.* 1. **誓詞 /誓詞, 宣誓** — A solemn pledge or promise that invokes a deity, a ruler, or another entity (not necessarily present) to attest the truth of a statement or sincerity of one's desire to fulfill a contract or promise.
2. **誓言** — A statement or promise which is strengthened (affirmed) by such a pledge.
3. **罵人話 /罵人話, 罵人 /罵人** — A curse, a curse word.
4. profane or obscene expression usually of surprise or anger
*“expletives were deleted”*
5. a commitment to tell the truth (especially in a court of law); to lie under oath is to become subject to prosecution for perjury
6. a solemn promise, usually invoking a divine witness, regarding your future acts or behavior
*“they took an oath of allegiance”*

<a id="entry-o-000027"></a>
## oatmeal

**發音：** /ˈoʊtmil/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **燕麥片 /燕麥片, 燕麥粥 /燕麥粥** — A breakfast cereal made from rolled oats, cooked in milk or water.
2. porridge made of rolled oats
3. meal made from rolled or ground oats

<a id="entry-o-000028"></a>
## oats

**發音：** /əʊts/
*n.* 1. *(plural, singular)* **燕麥 /燕麥** — Seeds of an oat plant, especially prepared as food.

*“the child's misery would move even the most obdurate heart”*

<a id="entry-o-000036"></a>
## obedience

**發音：** /ə(ʊ)ˈbiːdɪəns/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **百依百順 /百依百順, 服從 /服從** — The quality of being obedient.
2. the act of obeying; dutiful or submissive behavior with respect to another person
3. the trait of being willing to obey
4. behavior intended to please your parents
*“their children were never very strong on obedience”*
*“he went to law school out of respect for his father's wishes”*

2. dutifully complying with the commands or instructions of those in authority
*“an obedient soldier”*
*“obedient children”*

<a id="entry-o-000039"></a>
## obedient plant

*n.* 1. **一品香, 偽龍, 假龍頭花, 囊萼花, 棉鈴花** — North American plant having a spike of two-lipped pink or white flowers

## obelisk

*n.* 1. **方尖碑** — A tall, square, tapered, stone monolith topped with a pyramidal point, frequently used as a monument.
2. a stone pillar having a rectangular cross section tapering towards a pyramidal top
3. a character used in printing to indicate a cross reference or footnote

<a id="entry-o-000041"></a>
## obelus

**發音：** /ˈɒbɪləs/

<a id="entry-o-000042"></a>
## obese

2. **肥大+的, 肥胖+的, 胖** — excessively fat
*“a weighty man”*

<a id="entry-o-000043"></a>
## obesity

**發音：** /ə(ʊ)ˈbiːsɪti/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **肥胖, 肥胖症** — The state of being obese.
2. more than average fatness

<a id="entry-o-000044"></a>
## obey

*v.* 1. **聽從, 服從, 遵從** — be obedient to
2. *(transitive)* **服從 /服從, 遵守** — To do as ordered by (a person, institution etc), to act according to the bidding of.

<a id="entry-o-000045"></a>
## obfuscate

**發音：** /ˈɒbfəskeɪt/
*v.* 1. **弄暗** — To make dark; to overshadow.
2. **混淆, 弄亂 /弄亂, 搞亂 /搞亂** — To deliberately make more confusing in order to conceal the truth.
3. **混淆** — To alter code while preserving its behavior but concealing its structure and intent.
4. make obscure or unclear

<a id="entry-o-000046"></a>
## obi

**發音：** /ˈoʊbi/
*n.* 1. **寬腰帶 /寬腰帶** — A sash worn with a kimono.
2. **書腰 /書腰, 腰封** — A strip of paper looped around a book or other product.
3. (West Indies) followers of a religious system involving witchcraft and sorcery
4. a religious belief of African origin involving witchcraft and sorcery; practiced in parts of the West Indies and tropical Americas

<a id="entry-o-000047"></a>
## obidoxime chloride

*n.* 1. **雙復磷** — a chloride used as an antidote for nerve gases such as sarin or VX

<a id="entry-o-000048"></a>
## obiter dictum

**發音：** /ˌəʊbɪtə ˈdɪktəm/
*n.* 1. **附帶意見 /附帶意見, 附論 /附論, 附言** — An incidental remark; especially (law) a statement or remark in a court's judgment that is not essential to the disposition of the case.
2. an incidental remark
3. an opinion voiced by a judge on a point of law not directly bearing on the case in question and therefore not binding

<a id="entry-o-000049"></a>
## obituary

**發音：** /əˈbɪt͡ʃʊ(ə)ɹi/
*n.* 1. *(broadly)* **訃告 /訃告, 訃聞 /訃聞** — A brief biography of a person (especially one who is well-known) who has recently died, usually describing their life and achievements, particularly in the form of an article in a news publication or an item in a news broadcast.
2. a notice of someone's death; usually includes a short biography

<a id="entry-o-000050"></a>
## object

**發音：** /ˈɒb.d͡ʒɛkt/
*n.* 1. **客體, 對象, 物體, 目標** — a tangible and visible entity; an entity that can cast a shadow
*“it was full of rackets, balls and other objects”*
2. **目標, 目的** — the goal intended to be attained (and which is believed to be attainable)
*“the sole object of her trip was to see her children”*
3. **目標 /目標, 目的** — Objective; goal, end or purpose of something.
4. **物體 /物體, 物件, 東西 /東西, 事物** — A thing that has physical existence but is not alive.
5. **賓語 /賓語** — The noun phrase which is an internal complement of a verb phrase or a prepositional phrase. In a verb phrase with a transitive action verb, it is typically the receiver of the action.
6. **對象 /對象, 物件** — An instantiation of a class or structure.
*v.* 1. **反對, 拒絕** — express or raise an objection or protest or criticism or express dissent
*“She never objected to the amount of work her boss charged her with”*
*“When asked to drive the truck, she objected that she did not have a driver's license”*
2. *(intransitive)* **反對 /反對** — To disagree with or oppose something or someone; (especially in a Court of Law) to raise an objection.
3. **不喜歡, 反對, 抱反感, 拒絕** — be averse to or express disapproval of
*“My wife objects to modern furniture”*

<a id="entry-o-000051"></a>
## object permanence

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **物體恆存 /物體恆存** — The understanding (typically developed during early infancy) that an object still exists even when it disappears from sight, or other senses.

<a id="entry-o-000052"></a>
## object-based programming

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **基於對象編程 /基於對象編程** — A programming paradigm that encapsulates states and operations inside "objects" to design applications and computer programs.

<a id="entry-o-000053"></a>
## object-oriented

**發音：** /ˈɒbdʒɛkt ɔɹɪəntɪd/
*adj.* 1. **面向對象 /面向對象** — Using entities called objects that can process data and exchange messages with other objects.

<a id="entry-o-000054"></a>
## object-oriented language

*n.* 1. **面向對象語言 /面嚮對象語言** — Any programming language that encapsulates the state and operations inside objects that support inheritance.

<a id="entry-o-000055"></a>
## object-oriented programming

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **面向對象程序設計 /面向對象程序設計, 面向對象編程 /面向對象編程** — A programming paradigm that uses hierarchies of objects, instantiated from templates called classes, to design applications and computer programs.
2. creating a program that can use and support objects

<a id="entry-o-000056"></a>
## objectification

**發音：** /ɒbˌd͡ʒɛkt.ɪ.fɪˈkeɪ.ʃʌn/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **物化, 客體化 /客體化, 對象化 /對象化** — The act or process of objectifying something intangible.
2. the act of representing an abstraction as a physical thing
3. a concrete representation of an abstract idea or principle

<a id="entry-o-000057"></a>
## objectify

**發音：** /əbˈd͡ʒɛktɪˌfaɪ/
*v.* 1. *(ambitransitive)* **具象化** — To make (something, such as an abstract idea) possible to be perceived by the senses.
2. **使失去個性, 使客觀化** — make impersonal or present as an object
*“Will computers depersonalize human interactions?”*
*“Pornography objectifies women”*
3. *(transitive)* **物化** — To treat (someone) as a mere (usually sexual) object; to sexually dehumanize (someone).
4. make external or objective, or give reality to
*“language externalizes our thoughts”*

<a id="entry-o-000058"></a>
## objection

**發音：** /əbˈd͡ʒɛkʃən/
*n.* 1. **異議, 抗議, 反對** — the speech act of objecting
2. **反對, 異議, 抗議** — the act of protesting; a public (often organized) manifestation of dissent
3. **異議 /異議** — The act of objecting.
4. **異議 /異議** — A statement expressing opposition, or a reason or cause for expressing opposition (generally followed by the adposition to).
5. the act of expressing earnest opposition or protest
6. (law) a procedure whereby a party to a suit says that a particular line of questioning or a particular witness or a piece of evidence or other matter is improper and should not be continued and asks the court to rule on its impropriety or illegality

<a id="entry-o-000059"></a>
## objective

**發音：** /ɒbˈd͡ʒɛk.tɪv/
*n.* 1. **目標, 目的** — the goal intended to be attained (and which is believed to be attainable)
*“the sole object of her trip was to see her children”*
2. **目標 /目標, 目的** — A goal that is striven for.
3. **賓位 /賓位, 賓格 /賓格** — The objective case.
4. **物鏡 /物鏡** — The lens or lenses of a camera, microscope, or other optical device closest to the object being examined.
5. the lens or system of lenses in a telescope or microscope that is nearest the object being viewed
*adj.* 1. **不帶感情+的, 客觀上+的, 無個人偏見+的, 客觀+的** — undistorted by emotion or personal bias; based on observable phenomena
*“an objective appraisal”*
*“objective evidence”*
2. **客觀 /客觀** — Not influenced by the strong emotions or prejudices.
3. **賓格+的** — serving as or indicating the object of a verb or of certain prepositions and used for certain other purposes
*“objective case”*
*“accusative endings”*
4. **客觀+的, 紀實+的** — emphasizing or expressing things as perceived without distortion of personal feelings, insertion of fictional matter, or interpretation
*“objective art”*
5. belonging to immediate experience of actual things or events
*“objective benefits”*
*“an objective example”*

<a id="entry-o-000060"></a>
## objective case

*n.* 1. **賓格** — the case of nouns serving as the direct object of a verb

<a id="entry-o-000061"></a>
## objectively

*adv.* 1. **客觀+地** — with objectivity
*“we must look at the facts objectively”*

<a id="entry-o-000062"></a>
## objectivism

**發音：** /əbˈd͡ʒɛk.tɪ.vɪ.zəm/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **客觀主義 /客觀主義** — Any of several doctrines that holds that all of reality is objective and exists outside of the mind.

<a id="entry-o-000063"></a>
## objectivity

**發音：** /ˌɒbd͡ʒɛkˈtɪvɪti/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **客觀性 /客觀性** — The state of being objective, just, unbiased, and not influenced by irrational emotions or personal prejudices.
2. judgment based on observable phenomena and uninfluenced by emotions or personal prejudices

<a id="entry-o-000064"></a>
## oblanceolate

*adj.* 1. **倒披針形+的, 槍尖形+的** — (of a leaf shape) having a broad rounded apex and a tapering base

<a id="entry-o-000065"></a>
## oblate

*n.* 1. a lay person dedicated to religious work or the religious life
*adj.* 1. **扁圓+的, 扁平+的** — having the equatorial diameter greater than the polar diameter; being flattened at the poles

<a id="entry-o-000066"></a>
## obligate

*v.* 1. **使不得不, 責成, 強迫, 脅迫, 迫使** — force somebody to do something
*“We compel all students to fill out this form”*
2. **使負義務** — commit in order to fulfill an obligation
*“obligate money”*
3. bind by an obligation; cause to be indebted
*“He's held by a contract”*
*“I'll hold you by your promise”*
*adj.* 1. restricted to a particular condition of life
*“an obligate anaerobe can survive only in the absence of oxygen”*

<a id="entry-o-000067"></a>
## obligation

**發音：** /ˌɒb.lɪˈɡeɪ.ʃn̩/
*n.* 1. **勤務, 責任, 職責, 義務, 任務** — the social force that binds you to the courses of action demanded by that force
*“we must instill a sense of duty in our children”*
*“every right implies a responsibility; every opportunity, an obligation; every possession, a duty"- John D.Rockefeller Jr”*
2. **義務, 債務, 束縛, 職責, 責任** — the state of being obligated to do or pay something
*“he is under an obligation to finish the job”*
3. *(countable, uncountable)* **義務 /義務, 責任 /責任** — The act of binding oneself by a social, legal, or moral tie to someone.
4. **職責, 義務, 束縛, 責任** — a personal relation in which one is indebted for a service or favor
5. **債務證券** — a written promise to repay a debt
6. a legal agreement specifying a payment or action and the penalty for failure to comply

<a id="entry-o-000068"></a>
## obligational

*adj.* 1. **義務+的** — relating or constituting or qualified to create a legal or financial obligation
*“obligational authority”*

<a id="entry-o-000069"></a>
## obligatorily

*adv.* 1. **義務地, 強制地, 必須地** — in an obligatory manner
*“this rule applies obligatorily”*
2. in a manner that cannot be evaded
*“the ministry considers that contributions to such a fund should be met from voluntary donations rather than from rates compulsorily levied.”*

<a id="entry-o-000070"></a>
## oblige

3. *(intransitive, transitive)* **幫忙 /幫忙** — To do (someone) a service or favour (hence, originally, creating an obligation).
4. bind by an obligation; cause to be indebted

5. provide a service or favor for someone
*“We had to oblige him”*

*v.* 1. **妨礙, 阻塞, 攔阻, 阻礙** — hinder or prevent the progress or accomplishment of
*“His brother blocked him at every turn”*
2. **擋住 /擋住, 阻擋 /阻擋** — To block or fill (a passage) with obstacles or an obstacle.
3. **攔阻, 阻斷, 阻隔, 阻塞, 妨礙** — shut out from view or get in the way so as to hide from sight
*“The thick curtain blocked the action on the stage”*
*“The trees obstruct my view of the mountains”*
4. block passage through
*“obstruct the path”*

<a id="entry-o-000133"></a>
## obstruction

*n.* 1. **受阻, 妨礙** — the physical condition of blocking or filling a passage with an obstruction
2. any structure that makes progress difficult
3. something immaterial that stands in the way and must be circumvented or surmounted
*“lack of imagination is an obstacle to one's advancement”*
*“the poverty of a district is an obstacle to good education”*
4. the act of obstructing
*“obstruction of justice”*
5. getting in someone's way

<a id="entry-o-000134"></a>
## obstruction of justice

*n.* 1. *(US, countable, uncountable)* **妨害司法罪** — Interference with the administration of law and justice, as by ignorance of a justification, fraud of evidential validity, or not disclosing discovery of facts, or by doing damage to a witness, officer, juror.
2. impeding those who seek justice in a court (as by trying to influence or intimidate any juror or witness or officer of the court); can result in a finding of contempt of court

<a id="entry-o-000135"></a>

<a id="entry-o-000160"></a>

*“the boat was 5 miles off (or away)”*
*“the party is still 2 weeks off (or away)”*

*“clean off the dirt”*
*“he shaved off his mustache”*
*adj.* 1. **酸臭+的** — in an unpalatable state
*“sour milk”*
2. not in operation or operational
*“the oven is off”*
*“the lights are off”*
3. below a satisfactory level
*“an off year for tennis”*
*“his performance was off”*
4. (of events) no longer planned or scheduled
*“the wedding is definitely off”*
5. not performing or scheduled for duties
*“He's off every Tuesday”*
*v.* 1. **殘殺, 謀殺, 殺, 殺害** — kill intentionally and with premeditation
*“The mafia boss ordered his enemies murdered”*

## offence

*n.* 1. **攻勢, 進攻, 攻擊** — the action of attacking an enemy
2. **不恭, 傷感情, 冒犯, 失禮, 得罪** — a lack of politeness; a failure to show regard for others; wounding the feelings or others
3. **刑事犯罪, 罪行, 罪, 犯罪** — (criminal law) an act punishable by law; usually considered an evil act
*“a long record of crimes”*
4. the team that has the ball (or puck) and is trying to score
5. a feeling of anger caused by being offended
*“he took offence at my question”*

<a id="entry-o-000374"></a>
## offend

**發音：** /əˈfɛnd/
*v.* 1. **使激怒, 冒犯, 激怒** — cause to feel resentment or indignation
*“Her tactless remark offended me”*
2. *(transitive)* **冒犯, 觸犯 /觸犯, 得罪** — To hurt the feelings of; to displease; to make angry; to insult.
3. **犯罪, 違法** — act in disregard of laws, rules, contracts, or promises
*“offend all laws of humanity”*
*“violate the basic laws or human civilization”*
4. **使驚駭** — strike with disgust or revulsion
*“The scandalous behavior of this married woman shocked her friends”*
5. **傷, 傷害, 使受傷** — hurt the feelings of
*“She hurt me when she did not include me among her guests”*
*“This remark really bruised my ego”*

## offer

**發音：** /ˈɒfə(ɹ)/
*v.* 1. **提供** — make available or accessible, provide or furnish
*“The conference center offers a health spa”*
*“The hotel offers private meeting rooms”*
2. **奉送, 提供** — present for acceptance or rejection
*“She offered us all a cold drink”*
3. **自告奮勇, 自願** — agree freely
*“She volunteered to drive the old lady home”*
*“I offered to help with the dishes but the hostess would not hear of it”*
4. **表示** — offer verbally
*“extend my greetings”*
*“He offered his sympathy”*
5. **出價, 報價** — make available for sale
*“The stores are offering specials on sweaters this week”*
6. **出價, 投標, 報價** — propose a payment
*“The Swiss dealer offered $2 million for the painting”*
*n.* 1. **提供** — the verbal act of offering
*“a generous offer of assistance”*
2. **提供, 提議 /提議, 建議 /建議** — A proposal that has been made.
3. **試圖, 嘗試** — a usually brief attempt
*“he took a crack at it”*
*“I gave it a whirl”*
4. something offered (as a proposal or bid)
*“noteworthy new offerings for investors included several index funds”*

<a id="entry-o-000387"></a>
## offhand

*adj.* 1. **即席+的, 無準備+的** — with little or no preparation or forethought
*“his ad-lib comments showed poor judgment”*
*“an extemporaneous piano recital”*
2. casually thoughtless or inconsiderate
*“an offhand manner”*
*“she treated most men with offhand contempt”*
*adv.* 1. without previous thought or preparation
*“couldn't give the figures offhand”*
*“we decided offhand to go to Canada”*
2. in a casually inconsiderate manner
*“replied offhand, his mind a million miles away”*
*“she threw him over offhandedly without even a Dear-John letter”*

<a id="entry-o-000389"></a>
## office

**發音：** /ˈɒf.ɪs/
*n.* 1. **事務所, 辦公室** — place of business where professional or clerical duties are performed
*“he rented an office in the new building”*
2. **公職, 官職, 權力, 職務** — (of a government or government official) holding an office means being in power
*“being in office already gives a candidate a great advantage”*
*“during his first year in office”*
3. **職務 /職務, 職位 /職位** — A position of responsibility.
4. an administrative unit of government
*“the Central Intelligence Agency”*
*“the Census Bureau”*
5. the actions and activities assigned to or required or expected of a person or group
*“the function of a teacher”*
*“the government must do its part”*
6. professional or clerical workers in an office
*“the whole office was late the morning of the blizzard”*

<a id="entry-o-000390"></a>
## office automation

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **辦公自動化 /辦公自動化** — The varied computer machinery and software used to digitally create, collect, store, manipulate, and relay office information needed for accomplishing basic tasks.

<a id="entry-o-000391"></a>
## office building

*n.* 1. **寫字樓 /寫字樓, 辦公樓 /辦公樓, 辦公大樓 /辦公大樓** — A commercial building containing spaces for offices.
2. a building containing offices where work is done

<a id="entry-o-000392"></a>
## office chair

*n.* 1. **辦公椅 /辦公椅** — A chair that is designed for use at a desk in an office. It is generally comfortable and adjustable and can swivel 360 degrees and has wheels.

<a id="entry-o-000393"></a>
## office hours

*n.* 1. *(plural, plural-only)* **辦公時間 /辦公時間** — The times, typically from about 9am to 5pm, Monday to Friday, when non-24/7 office workers are at their desks.
2. *(plural, plural-only)* **諮詢時間 /諮詢時間** — A pre-arranged time when a person whose occupation frequently takes them away from their office during working hours is available in their office to answer questions or provide assistance without the requirement for an appointment.

<a id="entry-o-000394"></a>
## office worker

*n.* 1. **職員 /職員, 上班族** — Someone who works in an office.

<a id="entry-o-000395"></a>
## officer

**發音：** /ˈɒf.ɪ.sə/
*n.* 1. **警官, 警察** — a member of a police force
*“it was an accident, officer”*
2. **官員 /官員, 官吏** — One who has a position of authority in a hierarchical organization, especially in military, police or government organizations.
3. **官員 /官員** — One who holds a public office.
4. *(colloquial)* **軍官 /軍官** — A commissioned officer.
5. any person in the armed services who holds a position of authority or command
*“an officer is responsible for the lives of his men”*
6. someone who is appointed or elected to an office and who holds a position of trust
*“he is an officer of the court”*
*“the club elected its officers for the coming year”*
*v.* 1. direct or command as an officer

<a id="entry-o-000396"></a>
## official

**發音：** /əˈfɪʃəl/
*n.* 1. **做負責人或行使特殊使命的人, 公務員, 官, 官員, 職員** — a worker who holds or is invested with an office
2. **官員 /官員, 公務員 /公務員** — An office holder, a person holding an official position in government, sports, or other organization.
3. someone who administers the rules of a game or sport
*“the golfer asked for an official who could give him a ruling”*
*adj.* 1. **公務+的, 官方+的** — having official authority or sanction
*“official permission”*
*“an official representative”*
2. **官員+的** — of or relating to an office
*“official privileges”*
3. **官方的, 指定的, 正式的, 規定+的** — conforming to set usage, procedure, or discipline
*“in prescribed order”*
4. **官方, 正式** — Relating to an office, especially a subordinate executive officer or attendant.
5. verified officially
*“the election returns are now official”*
6. (of a church) given official status as a national or state institution

<a id="entry-o-000397"></a>
## official cover

*n.* 1. **官方臥底 /官方臥底** — A cover which is a position at a governmental agency (e.g. posing as a member of the diplomatic service).

<a id="entry-o-000398"></a>

**發音：** /əˈfɪʃəl ɡəˈzɛt/
*n.* 1. **公報 /公報, 憲報 /憲報** — A periodical publication that has been authorised to publish public or legal notices.

<a id="entry-o-000399"></a>
## official language

*n.* 1. **官方語言 /官方語言, 正式語文 /正式語文** — A language afforded an elevated status in a particular country, state, or other jurisdiction, typically for government use.

<a id="entry-o-000400"></a>
## officiate

*v.* 1. **主持主祭, 行使職務** — act in an official capacity in a ceremony or religious ritual, such as a wedding
*“Who officiated at your wedding?”*
2. perform duties attached to a particular office or place or function
*“His wife officiated as his private secretary”*

<a id="entry-o-000401"></a>
## officious

**發音：** /əˈfɪʃəs/
*adj.* 1. **多管閒事的** — Offensively intrusive or interfering in offering advice and services.
2. intrusive in a meddling or offensive manner
*“an interfering old woman”*
*“bustling about self-importantly making an officious nuisance of himself”*

<a id="entry-o-000402"></a>
## officiously

*adv.* 1. **多管閒事+地, 強行干預的** — in an officious manner
*“nothing so fatal as to strive too officiously for an abstract quality like beauty”*

<a id="entry-o-000403"></a>
## offing

*n.* 1. **不久的將來** — the near or foreseeable future
*“there was a wedding in the offing”*
2. the part of the sea that can be seen from the shore and is beyond the anchoring area
*“there was a ship in the offing”*

<a id="entry-o-000404"></a>
## offline

**發音：** /ɒfˈlaɪn/
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **脫機 /脫機, 離線 /離線** — Of a system, currently not connected (generally electrically) to a larger network. For example, a power plant which is not connected to the grid, or a computer which is not connected to the Internet or to any other communications service.

<a id="entry-o-000405"></a>
## offload

*v.* 1. **卸載** — transfer to a peripheral device, of computer data
2. take the load off (a container or vehicle)
*“unload the truck”*
*“offload the van”*

<a id="entry-o-000406"></a>
## offset

**發音：** /ˈɒf.sɛt/
*v.* 1. **抵消, 彌補, 補償** — compensate for or counterbalance
*“offset deposits and withdrawals”*
2. **彌補, 抵銷, 補救, 補償** — make up for
*“His skills offset his opponent's superior strength”*
3. cause (printed matter) to transfer or smear onto another surface
4. create an offset in
*“offset a wall”*
5. produce by offset printing
*“offset the conference proceedings”*
*n.* 1. **補償, 抵銷** — Anything that acts as counterbalance; a compensating equivalent.
2. **起始時間, 起始的時間, 開始, 開端** — the time at which something is supposed to begin
*“they got an early start”*
*“she knew from the get-go that he was the man for her”*
3. **抵銷貿易** — A form of countertrade arrangement, in which the seller agrees to purchase within a set time frame products of a certain value from the buying country. This kind of agreement may be used in large international public sector contracts such as arms sales.
4. **抵消金** — a compensating equivalent
5. **匍匐莖, 纖匐枝** — a horizontal branch from the base of plant that produces new plants from buds at its tips
6. **偏移** — The difference between a target memory address and a base address.

<a id="entry-o-000407"></a>
## offset printing

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **平版印刷** — Printing by the offset process, in which ink is carried from a metal plate to a rubber blanket (“offset cylinder”) and from there to the printing surface.
2. a plate makes an inked impression on a rubber-blanketed cylinder, which in turn transfers it to the paper

<a id="entry-o-000408"></a>
## offsetting balance

*n.* 1. **最低應存數, 補償餘額** — a minimum credit balance that a bank may require a borrower to keep on deposit as a condition for granting a loan; a common requirement for establishing a line of credit at a bank
*“the compensating balance increases the effective interest rate to the bank since the net amount loaned is reduced but the interest paid is unchanged”*

<a id="entry-o-000409"></a>
## offshoot

*n.* 1. **分枝** — Something which shoots off or separates from a main stem or branch of a plant.
2. **分支, 衍生物, 支流, 旁系** — Something which develops from something else.
3. a natural consequence of development

<a id="entry-o-000410"></a>
## offshore

**發音：** /ɒfˈʃɔː(ɹ)/
*adv.* 1. away from shore; away from land
*“cruising three miles offshore”*
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **離岸 /離岸, 濱外 /濱外, 國外 /國外** — Located in another country, especially one having beneficial tax laws or labor costs.
2. (of winds) coming from the land

3. at some distance from the shore
*“offshore oil reserves”*
*“an offshore island”*

<a id="entry-o-000412"></a>
## offside

**發音：** /ɒfˈsaɪd/
*n.* 1. **越位** — An offside play.

*adj.* 1. illegally beyond a prescribed line or area or ahead of the ball or puck
*“the touchdown was nullified because the left tackle was offside”*
*adv.* 1. **越位地** — illegally in advance of the ball or puck

<a id="entry-o-000413"></a>
## offspring

**發音：** /ˈɒfspɹɪŋ/
*n.* 1. **後嗣, 後裔, 子孫, 後代** — the immediate descendants of a person
*“she was the mother of many offspring”*
*“he died without issue”*
2. **子孫 /子孫, 兒孫 /兒孫** — Any of a person's descendants, including of further generations.
3. **崽, 幼畜** — any immature animal

*“this skyscraper is the solid materialization of his efforts”*

## ohm

**發音：** /əʊm/
*n.* 1. **歐姆 /歐姆** — In the International System of Units, the derived unit of electrical resistance; the electrical resistance of a device across which a potential difference of one volt causes a current of one ampere.
2. **歐姆** — a unit of electrical resistance equal to the resistance between two points on a conductor when a potential difference of one volt between them produces a current of one ampere

<a id="entry-o-000434"></a>
## Ohm's law

*prop. n.* 1. **歐姆定律 /歐姆定律** — Ohm's observation that the direct current flowing in an electrical circuit consisting only of resistances is directly proportional to the voltage applied.

## one

**發音：** /wʌn/
*adj.* 1. **一** — used of a single unit or thing; not two or more
*“`ane' is Scottish”*
2. **整體+的** — having the indivisible character of a unit
*“a unitary action”*
*“spoke with one voice”*
3. **同一+的, 一致+的** — of the same kind or quality
*“two animals of one species”*
4. used informally as an intensifier
*“that is one fine dog”*
5. indefinite in time or position
*“he will come one day”*
*“one place or another”*
6. being a single entity made by combining separate components
*“three chemicals combining into one solution”*
*n.* 1. **數目1** — the smallest whole number or a numeral representing this number
*“he has the one but will need a two and three to go with it”*
*“they had lunch at one”*

3. a single person or thing

## only

**發音：** /ˈoʊn.li/
*adv.* 1. **僅, 僅僅, 只不過, 只是, 只有** — and nothing more
*“I was merely asking”*
*“it is simply a matter of time”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **只有, 惟獨 /惟獨** — Without others or anything further; exclusively.
3. *(not-comparable)* **只有, 只** — No more than; just.
4. without any others being included or involved
*“was entirely to blame”*
*“a school devoted entirely to the needs of problem children”*
5. with nevertheless the final result
*“He arrived only to find his wife dead”*
*“We won only to lose again in the next round”*
6. in the final outcome
*“These news will only make you more upset”*
*adj.* 1. **唯一的, 單獨+的** — exclusive of anyone or anything else
*“she alone believed him”*
*“cannot live by bread alone”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **獨生的 /獨生的, 唯一的** — Without sibling; without a sibling of the same gender.
3. *(not-comparable)* **唯一** — Alone in a category.
4. **唯一+的** — being the only one; single and isolated from others
*“the lone doctor in the entire county”*
*“a lonesome pine”*
5. *(not-comparable)* **獨一無二的 /獨一無二的, 最好的** — Singularly superior; the best.

<a id="entry-o-000980"></a>
## open

*v.* 1. **開, 打開** — cause to open or to become open
*“Mary opened the car door”*
2. **開始** — start to operate or function or cause to start operating or functioning
*“open a business”*
3. **開始** — begin or set in action, of meetings, speeches, recitals, etc.
*“He opened the meeting with a long speech”*
4. **通往, 通向** — have an opening or passage or outlet
*“The bedrooms open into the hall”*
5. **開放, 打開** — afford access to
*“the door opens to the patio”*
*“The French doors give onto a terrace”*
6. **打開** — display the contents of a file or start an application as on a computer
*adj.* 1. **開著+的, 開放+的** — affording free passage or access
*“open drains”*
*“the road is open to traffic”*
2. **公開+的, 開放+的, 開闊+的, 自由出入+的, 除去障礙+的** — accessible to all
*“open season”*
*“an open economy”*
3. **無法防禦+的, 無防備+的** — not defended or capable of being defended
*“an open city”*
*“open to attack”*
4. **寬鬆+的** — (of textures) full of small openings or gaps
*“an open texture”*
*“a loose weave”*
5. **未定+的, 未確定+的, 未解決+的, 未決定+的** — not brought to a conclusion; subject to further thought
*“an open question”*
*“our position on this bill is still undecided”*
6. **開+的, 打開+的** — not sealed or having been unsealed
*“the letter was already open”*
*“the opened package lay on the table”*
*n.* 1. a clear or unobstructed space or expanse of land or water
*“finally broke out of the forest into the open”*
2. where the air is unconfined
*“he wanted to get outdoors a little”*
*“the concert was held in the open air”*
3. a tournament in which both professionals and amateurs may play
4. information that has become public
*“all the reports were out in the open”*
*“the facts had been brought to the surface”*

<a id="entry-o-000981"></a>

*n.* 1. **貸方賬, 賒購賬** — credit extended by a business to a customer
2. an unpaid credit order

## opportunism

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **機會主義 /機會主義, 投機 /投機, 投機主義 /投機主義** — The practice of taking advantage of any situations or people to achieve an end, often with no regard for principles or consequences.
2. *(Marxism, countable, uncountable)* **機會主義 /機會主義** — The political line of Communist Party members who zealously support the Party, but whose trait is that they yield to the mood of the moment, they are unable to resist what is fashionable, leading to the sacrifice of the permanent and essential interests of the Party to the momentary and minor interests.
3. taking advantage of opportunities without regard for the consequences for others

<a id="entry-o-001109"></a>

<a id="entry-o-001113"></a>
## oppose

**發音：** /əˈpəʊz/
*v.* 1. **抗爭, 反對** — be against; express opposition to
*“We oppose the ban on abortion”*
2. **勢均力敵** — contrast with equal weight or force
3. **反對** — To object to.
4. **起反作用, 抗爭, 反對** — act against or in opposition to
*“She reacts negatively to everything I say”*
5. **阻止, 對抗 /對抗** — To attempt to stop the progression of; to resist or antagonize by physical means, or by arguments, etc.; to contend against.
6. fight against or resist strongly
*“The senator said he would oppose the bill”*
*“Don't fight it!”*

<a id="entry-o-001114"></a>

*“two bitterly opposed schools of thought”*

## opposer

*n.* 1. **對手, 敵手** — someone who offers opposition

<a id="entry-o-001116"></a>
## opposite

**發音：** /ˈɒp.ə.zɪt/
*adj.* 1. **對面+的** — being directly across from each other; facing
*“And I on the opposite shore will be, ready to ride and spread the alarm"- Longfellow”*
*“we lived on opposite sides of the street”*
2. **相反+的** — moving or facing away from each other
*“looking in opposite directions”*
*“they went in opposite directions”*
3. **對應+的, 對立+的, 相對+的** — the other one of a complementary pair
*“the opposite sex”*
*“the two chess kings are set up on squares of opposite colors”*
4. **對立+的, 截然相反+的, 相反+的** — altogether different in nature or quality or significance
*“the medicine's effect was opposite to that intended”*
*“it is said that opposite characters make a union happiest"- Charles Reade”*
5. **完全不同+的, 正好相反+的, 截然相反+的** — characterized by opposite extremes; completely opposed
*“in diametric contradiction to his claims”*
*“diametrical (or opposite) points of view”*
6. *(not-comparable)* **對面的 /對面的** — Located directly across from something else, or from each other.
*n.* 1. **反義詞** — a word that expresses a meaning opposed to the meaning of another word, in which case the two words are antonyms of each other
*“to him the antonym of `gay' was `depressed'”*
2. **相反, 反面, 背面** — a relation of direct opposition
*“we thought Sue was older than Bill but just the reverse was true”*
3. **相反** — An antonym.
4. **對立面** — something inverted in sequence or character or effect
*“when the direct approach failed he tried the inverse”*
5. a contestant that you are matched against
*adv.* 1. directly facing each other
*“the two photographs lay face-to-face on the table”*
*“lived all their lives in houses face-to-face across the street”*
*prep.* 1. **對面 /對面** — Facing, or across from.

3. write an orchestra score for

<a id="entry-o-001256"></a>
## orchestrated

*adj.* 1. **編管絃樂曲+的** — arranged for performance by an orchestra

<a id="entry-o-001257"></a>
## orchestration

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **配器** — The arrangement of music for performance by an orchestra.
2. an arrangement of a piece of music for performance by an orchestra or band
3. the act of arranging a piece of music for an orchestra and assigning parts to the different musical instruments
4. an arrangement of events that attempts to achieve a maximum effect
*“the skillful orchestration of his political campaign”*

<a id="entry-o-001258"></a>
## orchid

**發音：** /ˈɔː.kɪd/
*n.* 1. **蘭花 /蘭花** — A plant of the orchid family (Orchidaceae), bearing unusually-shaped flowers of beautiful colours.
2. any of numerous plants of the orchid family usually having flowers of unusual shapes and beautiful colors

<a id="entry-o-001259"></a>
## Orchid Island

*prop. n.* 1. **蘭嶼 /蘭嶼** — An island of Taitung County, Taiwan, in the Pacific Ocean (Philippine Sea), home of the Tao (Yami) indigenous people.

<a id="entry-o-001260"></a>
## orchid tree

*n.* 1. **玲甲花, 羊蹄甲** — small East Indian tree having orchid-like flowers and hard dark wood

<a id="entry-o-001261"></a>
## orchidalgia

*n.* 1. **睪丸痛** — pain in the testes

<a id="entry-o-001262"></a>
## orchil

*n.* 1. **紫色地衣染料, 紫色染料, 苔色素** — a purplish dye obtained from orchil lichens
2. any of various lecanoras that yield the dye archil

<a id="entry-o-001263"></a>
## orchitis

*n.* 1. **睪丸炎, 睪丸炎** — inflammation of one or both testes; characterized by pain and swelling

<a id="entry-o-001264"></a>
## orcinus

*n.* 1. **虎鯨屬** — killer whales

<a id="entry-o-001265"></a>
## orcinus orca

*n.* 1. **虎鯨, 逆戟鯨** — predatory black-and-white toothed whale with large dorsal fin; common in cold seas

<a id="entry-o-001266"></a>
## Orcus

**發音：** /ˈɔːrkəs/
*prop. n.* 1. *(Roman)* **俄耳枯斯** — The Etruscan and Roman god of the underworld.
2. **亡神星** — A plutino and planetoid, possibly a dwarf planet; sometimes referred to as the “anti-Pluto”. Its moon is Vanth.

<a id="entry-o-001267"></a>
## orda

*n.* 1. *(historical)* **斡耳朵** — A political and military organizational structure used by Turkic and Mongol peoples, sometimes referred to as a horde.

<a id="entry-o-001268"></a>
## ordain

**發音：** /ɔɹˈdeɪn/
*v.* 1. **制定, 命令, 規定** — order by virtue of superior authority; decree
*“The King ordained the persecution and expulsion of the Jews”*
*“the legislature enacted this law in 1985”*
2. **任聖職, 任命** — appoint to a clerical posts
*“he was ordained in the Church”*
3. **按立** — To admit into the ministry, for example as a priest, bishop, minister or Buddhist monk, or to authorize as a rabbi.
4. **任命** — invest with ministerial or priestly authority
*“The minister was ordained only last month”*
5. issue an order

<a id="entry-o-001269"></a>
## ordained

*adj.* 1. **指定+的, 約定+的, 規定+的** — fixed or established especially by order or command
*“at the time appointed (or the appointed time")”*
2. invested with ministerial or priestly functions
*“an ordained priest”*

<a id="entry-o-001270"></a>
## ordeal

**發音：** /ɔːˈdiːl/
*n.* 1. *(historical)* **神明裁判, 神判, 神裁** — A trial in which the accused was subjected to a dangerous test (such as ducking in water), divine authority deciding the guilt of the accused.
2. *(figuratively)* **煎熬, 磨難 /磨難** — A painful or trying experience.
3. a severe or trying experience
4. a primitive method of determining a person's guilt or innocence by subjecting the accused person to dangerous or painful tests believed to be under divine control; escape was usually taken as a sign of innocence

<a id="entry-o-001271"></a>
## ordeal bean

*n.* 1. **毒扁豆** — dark brown highly poisonous seed of the calabar-bean vine; source of physostigmine and used in native witchcraft

<a id="entry-o-001272"></a>
## order

**發音：** /ˈɔː.də/
*n.* 1. **社會等級, 階級, 階層, 規則** — established customary state (especially of society)
*“order ruled in the streets”*
*“law and order”*
2. **秩序, 整齊** — a condition of regular or proper arrangement
*“he put his desk in order”*
*“the machine is now in working order”*
3. **法令, 命令** — a legally binding command or decision entered on the court record (as if issued by a court or judge)
*“a friend in New Mexico said that the order caused no trouble out there”*
4. **俱樂部, 團體** — a formal association of people with similar interests
*“he joined a golf club”*
*“they formed a small lunch society”*
5. *(countable)* **順序 /順序, 次序, 秩序** — Arrangement, disposition, or sequence.
6. *(countable)* **命令** — A command.
*v.* 1. **使有秩序, 使有規則, 使有規律, 使規律化, 規定** — bring into conformity with rules or principles or usage; impose regulations
*“We cannot regulate the way people dress”*
*“This town likes to regulate”*
2. *(transitive)* **命令** — To issue a command to; to charge.
3. *(transitive)* **定購 /定購, 點菜 /點菜, 點餐** — To request some product or service; to secure by placing an order.
4. **任聖職, 任命** — appoint to a clerical posts
*“he was ordained in the Church”*
5. **安排, 排列, 整理** — arrange thoughts, ideas, temporal events
*“arrange my schedule”*
*“set up one's life”*
6. **排名, 評價, 評估** — assign a rank or rating to
*“how would you rank these students?”*
*“The restaurant is rated highly in the food guide”*

<a id="entry-o-001273"></a>

*n.* 1. **蟎目, 蟎蜱目** — mites and ticks

## order book

*n.* 1. **訂單薄 /訂單薄** — A book or ledger that lists customer orders, especially orders that have not yet been filled.
2. a printed copy of the order of the day
3. a book in which customers' orders are entered; usually makes multiple copies of the order

<a id="entry-o-001380"></a>
## order of battle

*n.* 1. **作戰序列** — The arrangement of units or other divisions of the armed forces in combat; specifically, the deployment plans of an enemy, or a written record of this.

<a id="entry-o-001381"></a>
## order of business

*n.* 1. **事情, 任務 /任務** — A task which needs to be completed.
2. **應辦事項, 議程** — a list of matters to be taken up (as at a meeting)
3. **議程 /議程** — The sequence in which tasks need to be completed in order of importance or priority; an agenda.

<a id="entry-o-001382"></a>
## order of magnitude

*n.* 1. **數量級 /數量級** — The class of scale or magnitude of any amount, where each class contains values of a fixed ratio (most often 10) to the class preceding it.
2. **量值** — a number assigned to the ratio of two quantities; two quantities are of the same order of magnitude if one is less than 10 times as large as the other; the number of magnitudes that the quantities differ is specified to within a power of 10
3. a degree in a continuum of size or quantity
*“it was on the order of a mile”*
*“an explosion of a low order of magnitude”*

<a id="entry-o-001383"></a>
## order of payment

*n.* 1. **匯票** — a document ordering the payment of money; drawn by one person or bank on another

<a id="entry-o-001384"></a>
## order of service

*n.* 1. **崇拜程序** — The schedule of events for a religious service or ceremony.
2. **程序表** — A document detailing such a schedule.

<a id="entry-o-001451"></a>
## ordered

*adj.* 1. **有秩序+的** — disposed or placed in a particular kind of order
*“the carefully arranged chessmen”*
*“haphazardly arranged interlobular septa”*
2. having a systematic arrangement; especially having elements succeeding in order according to rule
*“an ordered sequence”*
3. marked by an orderly, logical, and aesthetically consistent relation of parts
*“a coherent argument”*

<a id="entry-o-001452"></a>
## ordered pair

*n.* 1. **有序對 /有序對** — An object containing exactly two elements in a fixed order, so that, when the elements are different, exchanging them gives a different object. Notation: (a, b) or ⟨a,b⟩.

<a id="entry-o-001453"></a>
## ordered series

*n.* 1. **數值範圍** — an ordered reference standard
*“judging on a scale of 1 to 10”*

<a id="entry-o-001454"></a>
## orderliness

*n.* 1. **秩序, 整齊** — a condition of regular or proper arrangement
*“he put his desk in order”*
*“the machine is now in working order”*
2. the quality of appreciating method and system

<a id="entry-o-001455"></a>
## orderly

*adj.* 1. **安排好+的, 整潔+的, 有秩序+的, 整齊+的** — clean or organized
*“her neat dress”*
*“a neat room”*
2. devoid of violence or disruption
*“an orderly crowd confronted the president”*
*n.* 1. a soldier who serves as an attendant to a superior officer
*“the orderly laid out the general's uniform”*
2. a male hospital attendant who has general duties that do not involve the medical treatment of patients

<a id="entry-o-001456"></a>
## ordinal

*n.* 1. **序數, 順序數** — the number designating place in an ordered sequence
*adj.* 1. **分類順序+的, 依次+的, 順序+的** — of or relating to a taxonomic order
*“family and ordinal names of animals and plants”*
2. **依次+的, 順序+的** — being or denoting a numerical order in a series
*“ordinal numbers”*
*“held an ordinal rank of seventh”*

<a id="entry-o-001457"></a>
## ordinal indicator

*n.* 1. **序數指示符 /序數指示符** — A sign adjacent to a numeral denoting that it is an ordinal number, rather than a cardinal number.

<a id="entry-o-001458"></a>
## ordinal number

*n.* 1. **序數, 順序數** — the number designating place in an ordered sequence
2. **順序數 /順序數, 序數 /序數, 序數詞 /序數詞** — A natural number used to denote position in a sequence.

<a id="entry-o-001459"></a>
## ordinance

**發音：** /ˈɔːdɪnəns/
*n.* 1. **規則** — an authoritative rule
2. *(US)* **法令, 條令 /條令, 條例 /條例** — A local law, passed by e.g. a city.
3. a statute enacted by a city government
4. the act of ordaining; the act of conferring (or receiving) holy orders
*“the rabbi's family was present for his ordination”*

<a id="entry-o-001460"></a>
## ordinary

**發音：** /ˈɔːdɪnəɹi/
*adj.* 1. **平凡+的, 平常+的, 一般+的, 普通+的** — not exceptional in any way especially in quality or ability or size or degree
*“ordinary everyday objects”*
*“ordinary decency”*
2. **普通, 平常, 平凡** — Being part of the natural order of things; normal, customary, routine.
3. lacking special distinction, rank, or status; commonly encountered
*“average people”*
*“the ordinary (or common) man in the street”*
*n.* 1. **一般, 平凡, 平常, 普通** — the expected or commonplace condition or situation
*“not out of the ordinary”*
2. a judge of a probate court
3. a clergyman appointed to prepare condemned prisoners for death
4. an early bicycle with a very large front wheel and small back wheel
5. (heraldry) any of several conventional figures used on shields

<a id="entry-o-001461"></a>
## ordinary annuity

*n.* 1. **一般年金, 普通年金** — an annuity paid in a series of more or less equal payments at the end of equally spaced periods

<a id="entry-o-001462"></a>
## ordinary differential equation

*n.* 1. **常微分方程** — An equation involving the derivatives of a function of only one independent variable.

<a id="entry-o-001463"></a>
## ordinary life insurance

*n.* 1. **普通終身壽險, 終身人身保險** — insurance on the life of the insured for a fixed amount at a definite premium that is paid each year in the same amount during the entire lifetime of the insured

<a id="entry-o-001464"></a>
## ordinary shares

*n.* 1. **普通股** — stock other than preferred stock; entitles the owner to a share of the corporation's profits and a share of the voting power in shareholder elections
*“over 40 million Americans invest in common stocks”*

<a id="entry-o-001465"></a>
## ordinate

**發音：** /ˈɔː(ɹ)dɪnət/
*n.* 1. **縱坐標 /縱座標** — The second of the two terms by which a point is referred to, in a system of fixed rectilinear coordinate (Cartesian coordinate) axes.
2. the value of a coordinate on the vertical axis
*v.* 1. **任聖職, 任命** — appoint to a clerical posts
*“he was ordained in the Church”*
2. **協調** — bring (components or parts) into proper or desirable coordination correlation
*“align the wheels of my car”*
*“ordinate similar parts”*

<a id="entry-o-001466"></a>
## ordination

*n.* 1. **授聖職** — the status of being ordained to a sacred office
2. logical or comprehensible arrangement of separate elements
*“we shall consider these questions in the inverse order of their presentation”*
3. the act of ordaining; the act of conferring (or receiving) holy orders
*“the rabbi's family was present for his ordination”*

2. *(countable, uncountable)* **大砲 /大炮** — Artillery.
3. **大炮, 火炮** — large but transportable armament
4. military supplies

<a id="entry-o-001488"></a>
## organ

**發音：** /ˈɔːɡən/
*n.* 1. **器官** — a fully differentiated structural and functional unit in an animal that is specialized for some particular function
2. **電子琴** — (music) an electronic simulation of a pipe organ
3. **器官** — The larger part of an organism, composed of tissues that perform similar functions.
4. *(broadly)* **機關 /機關, 機構 /機構** — A body of an organization dedicated to the performing of certain functions.
5. **風琴 /風琴** — A musical instrument that has multiple pipes which play when a key is pressed (the pipe organ), or an electronic instrument designed to replicate such.
6. **管風琴, 風琴** — wind instrument whose sound is produced by means of pipes arranged in sets supplied with air from a bellows and controlled from a large complex musical keyboard

<a id="entry-o-001489"></a>
## organ console

*n.* 1. **風琴控制臺 /風琴控制檯** — The manuals, the pedals and associated stops of an organ, especially a pipe organ.

<a id="entry-o-001498"></a>
## organic

**發音：** /ɔːˈɡænɪk/

*“organic life”*
*“organic growth”*

*“organic vegetables”*
6. relating or belonging to the class of chemical compounds having a carbon basis
*“hydrocarbons are organic compounds”*

<a id="entry-o-001513"></a>
## organisation

*n.* 1. **組織** — the persons (or committees or departments etc.) who make up a body for the purpose of administering something
*“he claims that the present administration is corrupt”*
*“the governance of an association is responsible to its members”*
2. **團體, 集團, 組織, 機構** — a group of people who work together
3. **組織** — an organized structure for arranging or classifying
*“he changed the arrangement of the topics”*
*“the facts were familiar but it was in the organization of them that he was original”*
4. an ordered manner; orderliness by virtue of being methodical and well organized
*“his compulsive organization was not an endearing quality”*
*“we can't do it unless we establish some system around here”*
5. the act of organizing a business or an activity related to a business
*“he was brought in to supervise the organization of a new department”*
6. the activity or result of distributing or disposing persons or things properly or methodically
*“his organization of the work force was very efficient”*

<a id="entry-o-001514"></a>
## organisational

*adj.* 1. **組織+的** — of or relating to an organization
*“organizational structure”*

<a id="entry-o-001515"></a>
## organise

*v.* 1. **有機化組織, 組織, 有機化** — bring order and organization to
*“Can you help me organize my files?”*
2. **使組成, 有機化, 給予生機, 組織** — create (as an entity)
*“social groups form everywhere”*
*“They formed a company”*
3. **加入工會, 組成工會, 組織** — form or join a union
*“The auto workers decided to unionize”*
4. **有機化, 組織** — cause to be structured or ordered or operating according to some principle or idea
5. **指導, 策劃, 設計, 管理, 組織** — plan and direct (a complex undertaking)
*“he masterminded the robbery”*
6. arrange by systematic planning and united effort
*“machinate a plot”*
*“organize a strike”*

<a id="entry-o-001516"></a>
## organised

*adj.* 1. **有組織+的** — being a member of or formed into a labor union
*“organized labor”*
*“unionized workers”*

## original

*“a truly original approach”*

*“he kept the original copy and gave her only a xerox”*

2. **原作, 原文, 原物, 本來 /本來, 原著, 原件** — An object or other creation (e.g. narrative work) from which all later copies and variations are derived.
3. something that serves as a model or a basis for making copies
*“this painting is a copy of the original”*

## osmosis

**發音：** /ɒzˈməʊ̯sɪs/
*n.* 1. **滲透作用, 滲透** — (biology, chemistry) diffusion of molecules through a semipermeable membrane from a place of higher concentration to a place of lower concentration until the concentration on both sides is equal

3. *(countable, figuratively, uncountable)* **耳濡目染** — Passive absorption or impartation of information, habits, etc.; the process of teaching or learning particular knowledge incidentally rather than consciously.

<a id="entry-o-001717"></a>
## osmotic pressure

*adv.* 1. **誇示+地, 誇耀地, 招搖地, 炫耀地, 鋪張地** — with ostentation; in an ostentatious manner
*“Mr Khrushchev ostentatiously wooed and embraced Castro at the U.N. general assembly”*

<a id="entry-o-001746"></a>
## osteoarthritis

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **骨關節炎 /骨關節炎** — A form of arthritis, affecting mainly older people, caused by chronic degeneration of the cartilage and synovial membrane of the joints, leading to pain and stiffness.
2. **骨關節炎** — chronic breakdown of cartilage in the joints; the most common form of arthritis occurring usually after middle age

<a id="entry-o-001747"></a>
## osteoblast

*n.* 1. **成骨細胞 /成骨細胞** — A mononucleate cell from which bone develops.
2. a cell from which bone develops

<a id="entry-o-001748"></a>
## osteoblastoma

*n.* 1. **成骨細胞瘤** — benign tumor of bone and fibrous tissue; occurs in the vertebrae or femur or tibia or arm bones (especially in young adults)

<a id="entry-o-001749"></a>
## osteochondrodysplasia

**發音：** /ˈɒs.ti.əʊˌkɒn.drə(ʊ)ˌdɪs.pleɪ.ʒə/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **骨軟骨發育不良 /骨軟骨發育不良** — A group of genetic disorders affecting the development and growth of bone and cartilage, leading to abnormal skeletal formation and growth abnormalities.

<a id="entry-o-001750"></a>
## osteochondroma

*n.* 1. **軟骨骨瘤** — benign tumor containing both bone and cartilage; usually occurs near the end of a long bone

<a id="entry-o-001751"></a>
## osteochondrosis

**發音：** /ˌɒstiːəʊkɒnˈdɹəʊsɪs/
*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **骨軟骨炎 /骨軟骨炎** — A family of orthopedic diseases of the joint that occur in children and in rapidly growing animals, characterized by interruption of the blood supply of a bone, necrosis, and regrowth.

<a id="entry-o-001752"></a>
## osteodystrophy

*n.* 1. **骨營養不良, 骨營養障礙, 骨質營養不良** — defective bone development; usually attributable to renal disease or to disturbances in calcium and phosphorus metabolism

<a id="entry-o-001753"></a>
## osteogenesis imperfecta

*n.* 1. *(uncountable)* **成骨不全症** — A hereditary disease characterized by brittle bones that easily fracture.
2. **成骨不全症** — autosomal dominant disorder of connective tissue characterized by brittle bones that fracture easily

<a id="entry-o-001754"></a>
## osteogenic sarcoma

*n.* 1. **骨肉瘤** — malignant bone tumor; most common in children and young adults where it tends to affect the femur

<a id="entry-o-001755"></a>
## osteology

*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **骨學 /骨學** — The scientific study of the morphology and pathology of bones.
2. the branch of anatomy that studies the bones of the vertebrate skeleton

<a id="entry-o-001756"></a>
## osteolysis

*n.* 1. **溶骨作用, 骨熔, 骨質溶解, 骨軟化** — lysis of bone caused by disease or infection or inadequate blood supply

<a id="entry-o-001757"></a>
## osteoma

*n.* 1. **骨瘤** — A benign bony tumour that typically grows on the skull.
2. **骨瘤** — a slow growing benign tumor of consisting of bone tissue; usually on the skull or mandible

<a id="entry-o-001758"></a>
## osteomalacia

*n.* 1. **軟骨病** — abnormal softening of bones caused by deficiencies of phosphorus or calcium or vitamin D

<a id="entry-o-001759"></a>
## osteomyelitis

*n.* 1. **骨髓炎** — an inflammation of bone and bone marrow (usually caused by bacterial infection)

<a id="entry-o-001760"></a>
## osteopenia

*n.* 1. *(countable, uncountable)* **骨質缺乏症 /骨質缺乏症** — The medical condition of having low bone density, but not low enough to be considered osteoporosis.

<a id="entry-o-001761"></a>
## osteopetrosis

*n.* 1. **骨石化病** — an inherited disorder characterized by an increase in bone density; in severe forms the bone marrow cavity may be obliterated

<a id="entry-o-001762"></a>
## osteophyte

**發音：** /ˈɒstɪəfʌɪt/
*n.* 1. **骨贅 /骨贅** — A small, abnormal growth of bone (exostosis, bone spur) that forms along joint margins.
2. small abnormal bony outgrowth

<a id="entry-o-001763"></a>
## osteoporosis

**發音：** /ˌɒstiəʊpəˈɹəʊsɪs/
*n.* 1. **骨質疏鬆, 骨質疏鬆症** — abnormal loss of bony tissue resulting in fragile porous bones attributable to a lack of calcium; most common in postmenopausal women
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **骨質疏鬆 /骨質疏鬆** — A disease, occurring especially in women following menopause, in which the bones become extremely porous and are subject to fracture.

<a id="entry-o-001764"></a>
## osteosarcoma

*n.* 1. **骨肉瘤** — A type of cancer of the bone.
2. **骨肉瘤** — malignant bone tumor; most common in children and young adults where it tends to affect the femur

<a id="entry-o-001765"></a>
## osteosclerosis

*n.* 1. **骨硬化病** — abnormal hardening or eburnation of bone

<a id="entry-o-001766"></a>

*n.* 1. **軟骨發育不全** — an inherited skeletal disorder beginning before birth; cartilage is converted to bone resulting in dwarfism

<a id="entry-o-001767"></a>
## osteotomy

*n.* 1. **截骨術 /截骨術** — The surgical procedure of cutting a bone. This is often performed to either lengthen, shorten, or straighten a bone.
2. surgical sectioning of bone

<a id="entry-o-001768"></a>

2. **孤立, 排斥** — avoid speaking to or dealing with
*“Ever since I spoke up, my colleagues ostracize me”*

<a id="entry-o-001772"></a>
## ostracism

**發音：** /ˈɒstɹəsɪz(ə)m/
*n.* 1. **放逐, 驅逐** — the state of being banished or ostracized (excluded from society by general consent)
*“the association should get rid of its elderly members--not by euthanasia, of course, but by Coventry”*
2. *(countable, uncountable)* **放逐** — Temporary exclusion from a community or society.
3. *(countable, historical, uncountable)* **陶片放逐制** — In ancient Athens (and some other cities), the temporary banishment by popular vote of a citizen considered dangerous to the state.
4. the act of excluding someone from society by general consent

<a id="entry-o-001773"></a>
## ostracize

**發音：** /ˈɒstɹəsaɪz/
*v.* 1. **放逐, 排斥, 驅逐** — expel from a community or group
2. *(British, English, Oxford)* **放逐, 流放** — To exclude a person from a community or from society by not communicating with them or by refusing to acknowledge their presence; to refuse to associate with or talk to; to shun.
3. **孤立, 排斥** — avoid speaking to or dealing with
*“Ever since I spoke up, my colleagues ostracize me”*

<a id="entry-o-001798"></a>
## other

**發音：** /ˈʌðə/
*adj.* 1. **其他+的, 其它, 另外+的** — not the same one or ones already mentioned or implied
*“today isn't any other day"- the White Queen”*
*“the construction of highways and other public works”*
2. *(not-comparable)* **別的 /別的, 其他的** — Different.
3. recently past
*“the other evening”*
4. belonging to the distant past
*“the early inhabitants of Europe”*
*“former generations”*
5. very unusual; different in character or quality from the normal or expected
*“a strange, other dimension...where his powers seemed to fail"- Lance Morrow”*
*det.* 1. **其他, 別 /別** — Not the one or ones previously referred to.

<a id="entry-o-001799"></a>
## other than

*adv.* 1. in another and different manner
*“very soon you will know differently”*
*“she thought otherwise”*
*prep.* 1. *(idiomatic)* **除了...以外** — Except, besides.

<a id="entry-o-001800"></a>
## other woman

*n.* 1. *(idiomatic, informal)* **小三, 第三者** — A woman who is romantically involved with someone (usually a man) already in a committed relationship with another woman, especially a woman having an affair with a married man.

<a id="entry-o-001801"></a>
## othering

**發音：** /ˈʌðəɹɪŋ/

<a id="entry-o-001802"></a>
## others

**發音：** /ˈʌðəz/
*n.* 1. *(plural, plural-only)* **其他人, 別人 /別人** — Other people.
2. *(plural, plural-only)* **其他** — Those remaining after one or more people or items have left, or done something else, or been excluded.

<a id="entry-o-001803"></a>
## otherwise

**發音：** /ˈʌð.əˌwaɪz/
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **反之** — Differently, in another way.
2. *(conjunctive, not-comparable)* **否則 /否則** — In different circumstances; or else.
3. *(conjunctive, not-comparable)* **除此以外, 除此之外** — In all other respects; apart from that; except for this.
4. in other respects or ways
*“he is otherwise normal”*
*“the funds are not otherwise available”*
5. in another and different manner
*“very soon you will know differently”*
*“she thought otherwise”*
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **矛盾的** — Other than supposed; different.
2. **別+的, 另外+的, 不同+的** — other than as supposed or expected
*“the outcome was otherwise”*

*“otiose lines in a play”*
*“advice is wasted words”*

*“the therapy was ineffectual”*

<a id="entry-o-001856"></a>
## out

**發音：** /ˈäʊ̯t/
*adv.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **出** — Away from the inside or centre.
2. away from home
*“they went out last night”*
3. moving or appearing to move away from a place, especially one that is enclosed or hidden
*“the cat came out from under the bed”*
4. from one's possession
*“he gave out money to the poor”*
*“gave away the tickets”*
*adj.* 1. **出局+的** — not allowed to continue to bat or run
*“he was tagged out at second on a close play”*
*“he fanned out”*
2. **出局+的** — out of power; especially having been unsuccessful in an election
*“now the Democrats are out”*
3. being out or having grown cold
*“threw his extinct cigarette into the stream”*
*“the fire is out”*
4. not worth considering as a possibility
*“a picnic is out because of the weather”*
5. excluded from use or mention
*“forbidden fruit”*
*“in our house dancing and playing cards were out”*
6. directed outward or serving to direct something outward
*“the out doorway”*
*“the out basket”*
*n.* 1. (baseball) a failure by a batter or runner to reach a base safely in baseball
*“you only get 3 outs per inning”*
*v.* 1. to state openly and publicly one's homosexuality
*“This actor outed last year”*
2. reveal (something) about somebody's identity or lifestyle
*“The gay actor was outed last week”*
*“Someone outed a CIA agent”*
3. be made known; be disclosed or revealed
*“The truth will out”*
*interj.* 1. **滾 /滾** — Get out; begone; away!

<a id="entry-o-001995"></a>
## over

**發音：** /ˈəʊ̯.və/
*adv.* 1. at or to a point across intervening space etc.
*“come over and see us some time”*
*“over there”*
2. throughout an area
*“he is known the world over”*
3. throughout a period of time
*“stay over the weekend”*
4. beyond the top or upper surface or edge; forward from an upright position
*“a roof that hangs over”*
5. over the entire area
*“the wallpaper was covered all over with flowers”*
*“she ached all over”*
*adj.* 1. *(not-comparable)* **了斷 /了斷, 結束 /結束, 終止 /終止** — Finished; ended; concluded.
2. having come or been brought to a conclusion
*“the harvesting was complete”*
*“the affair is over, ended, finished”*
*n.* 1. **一輪投球數** — (cricket) the division of play during which six balls are bowled at the batsman by one player from the other team from the same end of the pitch
*prep.* 1. **在……之上** — On top of; in such a way as to cover.
*interj.* 1. **完畢** — A radio procedure word meaning that the station is finished with its transmission and is expecting a response.

## ozone

**發音：** /ˈəʊzəʊn/
*n.* 1. *(uncountable, usually)* **臭氧** — An allotrope of oxygen (symbol O₃) having three atoms in the molecule instead of the usual two; it is a toxic gas, generated from oxygen by electrical discharge.
2. a colorless gas (O3) soluble in alkalis and cold water; a strong oxidizing agent; can be produced by electric discharge in oxygen or by the action of ultraviolet radiation on oxygen in the stratosphere (where it acts as a screen for ultraviolet radiation)
